**Formulas / laws:** Fractional uncertainty = absolute uncertainty / measured value; percentage uncertainty = (absolute uncertainty / measured value) x 100%; Addition/subtraction: absolute uncertainties add; multiplication/division: percentage uncertainties add; power: % uncertainty x power; Rounding rules (digit <5 unchanged, >5 increase, =5 round to even); Dimensions: [v]=[LT^-1], [a]=[LT^-2], [F]=[MLT^-2]; Homogeneity check of S = vi t + (1/2) a t^2; Dimensional derivation F = m v^2 / r (centripetal force); E = mc^2

**Formulas / laws:** Ax = A cos(theta), Ay = A sin(theta); A = sqrt(Ax^2 + Ay^2); tan(theta) = Ay/Ax; A.B = AB cos(theta) = AxBx + AyBy + AzBz; cos(theta) = A.B/(AB); A x B = AB sin(theta) n^; A x B = -B x A; vf = vi + at; S = vi t + (1/2) a t^2; 2aS = vf^2 - vi^2 (a -> g for free fall); Projectile: vx = vi cos(theta); vy = vi sin(theta) - g t; h = vi^2 sin^2(theta)/(2g); T = 2 vi sin(theta)/g; R = vi^2 sin(2 theta)/g; Rmax at 45 deg; p = mv; F t = mvf - mvi (impulse); F = change in momentum / time; m1v1 + m2v2 = m1v1' + m2v2' (conservation of momentum); Elastic 1-D: v1 - v2 = -(v1' - v2'); v1' = (m1-m2)v1/(m1+m2) + 2m2 v2/(m1+m2); v2' = 2m1 v1/(m1+m2) + (m2-m1)v2/(m1+m2); Perfectly inelastic: vf = (m1v1 + m2v2)/(m1+m2); 2-D collisions: x- and y-momentum conservation, KE conservation (elastic); KE loss = (KE)i - (KE)f (inelastic); Rocket: a = m v / M (thrust = rate of momentum of ejected gas)

**Formulas / laws:** z = a + ib, i^2 = -1; Re z = a, Im z = b; conjugate = a - ib; (a+ib)(c+id) = (ac - bd) + i(ad + bc); (a,b)(c,d) = (ac - bd, ad + bc); inverse of (a,b) = (a/(a^2+b^2), -b/(a^2+b^2)); |z| = sqrt(x^2 + y^2); |z|^2 = z * conj(z); sqrt(x+iy) = ±( sqrt((|z|+x)/2) + i (y/|y|) sqrt((|z|-x)/2) ); P(z) = a(z - z1)(z - z2)...(z - zn) (corollary of Fundamental Theorem of Algebra); cube roots of unity: 1, w = (-1 + i sqrt3)/2, w^2 = (-1 - i sqrt3)/2; 1 + w + w^2 = 0; w^3 = 1; fourth roots of unity: 1, -1, i, -i (sum 0, product -1); z = r(cos t + i sin t), r = |z|, t = tan^-1(y/x); Arg z in (-pi, pi]; z1 z2 = r1 r2 [cos(t1+t2) + i sin(t1+t2)]; z1/z2 = (r1/r2)[cos(t1-t2) + i sin(t1-t2)]; Arg(z1 z2) = Arg z1 + Arg z2; Arg(z1/z2) = Arg z1 - Arg z2; Ohm's law for AC: V = I Z, Z = R + iX, X = XL - XC

**Formulas / laws:** Charge on complex = metal oxidation state + sum of ligand charges, e.g. [Co(NH3)6]3+, [Fe(CN)6]4-, [Ni(CO)4]; dE = hv = hc/lambda (Planck); larger dE -> higher frequency absorbed; Octahedral: 3 lower (dxy,dxz,dyz) + 2 higher (dx2-y2, dz2); tetrahedral inverted and smaller; [Cu(H2O)6]2+ + 2OH- -> [Cu(H2O)4(OH)2] (pale blue ppt) + 2H2O; [Cu(H2O)6]2+ + 4NH3 -> [Cu(NH3)4(H2O)2]2+ (deep blue) + 4H2O; [Cu(H2O)6]2+ + 4Cl- <=> [CuCl4]2- (yellow-green) + 6H2O; Kstab = [CuCl4 2-]/([Cu(H2O)6 2+][Cl-]^4); [Co(H2O)6]2+ + 2OH- -> Co(OH)2 (blue ppt); + excess NH3 -> [Co(NH3)6]2+ (yellow); + conc HCl -> [CoCl4]2- (blue, tetrahedral); [Cu(H2O)6]2+ + 3en -> [Cu(en)3]2+ + 6H2O (chelate effect); log Kstab: [CuCl4]2- 5.6, [Cu(NH3)4]2+ 13.1, [Cu(EDTA)]2- 18.8; E cell = E cathode - E anode; feasible if positive; 2MnO4- + 5C2O4 2- + 16H+ -> 2Mn2+ + 10CO2 + 8H2O; MnO4- + 5Fe2+ + 8H+ -> Mn2+ + 5Fe3+ + 4H2O (E = +0.75 V); 2Cu2+ + 4I- -> 2CuI(s) + I2 (driven by CuI precipitation; I2 titrated with thiosulphate)

**Formulas / laws:** Mean (ungrouped) = Σx / n; Mean (grouped) = Σ(f·x) / Σf (x = class midpoint); Median: odd n -> value at (n+1)/2; even n -> average of values at n/2 and n/2+1; Mode (grouped) = l + [(fm - f1) / ((fm - f1) + (fm - f2))] × h; Standard deviation σ = sqrt[ Σ(x - μ)^2 / N ]; variance = σ^2; Range = Xmax - Xmin; Percentile rank R = (P/100) × (n + 1); interpolate if non-integer; Pie sector angle = (category value / total) × 360°

**Formulas / laws:** y = a(x-h)(x-k): x-intercepts h, k; vertex at x = (h+k)/2; y-intercept a*h*k; f(x) = a^x (a>0, a!=1): domain R, range (0,inf), y-intercept (0,1), asymptote y = 0; increasing if a>1, decreasing if 0<a<1; e = lim (1+1/n)^n ~ 2.71828; sinh x = (e^x - e^-x)/2, cosh x = (e^x + e^-x)/2, tanh x = sinh x/cosh x etc.; cosh^2 x - sinh^2 x = 1; 1 - tanh^2 x = sech^2 x; coth^2 x - 1 = csch^2 x; sinh 2x = 2 sinh x cosh x; cosh 2x = cosh^2 x + sinh^2 x; sinh^-1 x = ln(x + sqrt(x^2+1)); cosh^-1 x = ln(x + sqrt(x^2-1)), x>=1; tanh^-1 x = (1/2) ln((1+x)/(1-x)), |x|<1; coth^-1 x = (1/2) ln((x+1)/(x-1)), |x|>1; sech^-1 x, csch^-1 x log forms; y = log_a x <=> a^y = x; log_a(xy) = log_a x + log_a y; log_a(x/y) = log_a x - log_a y; log_a x^t = t log_a x; log_a x = log_b x / log_b a; |x| = x (x>=0), -x (x<0); Shift: y = f(x-h) + k; Scaling: y = a f(bx); N = N0 e^(kt) (k>0 growth, k<0 decay); L = 10 log(I/I0), I0 = 10^-12 W/m^2; A = P(1 + r/n)^(nt) (discrete); A = P e^(rt) (continuous)

**Formulas / laws:** d/dx(sin x)=cos x; d/dx(cos x)=-sin x; d/dx(tan x)=sec^2 x; d/dx(cot x)=-csc^2 x; d/dx(sec x)=sec x tan x; d/dx(csc x)=-csc x cot x; Chain rule: dy/dx = (dy/du)(du/dx); parametric: dy/dx = (dy/dt)/(dx/dt); d/dx sin^-1 x = 1/sqrt(1-x^2); d/dx cos^-1 x = -1/sqrt(1-x^2); d/dx tan^-1 x = 1/(1+x^2); d/dx cot^-1 x = -1/(1+x^2); d/dx sec^-1 x = 1/(|x| sqrt(x^2-1)); d/dx csc^-1 x = -1/(|x| sqrt(x^2-1)); d/dx a^x = a^x ln a; d/dx e^x = e^x; d/dx log_a x = 1/(x ln a); d/dx ln x = 1/x; Tangent: y - y0 = f'(x0)(x - x0); Normal: y - y0 = -(1/f'(x0))(x - x0); f'(x) >= 0 on I => increasing; f'(x) <= 0 => decreasing; Second derivative test: f'(c)=0, f''(c)<0 => relative max; f''(c)>0 => relative min; f''(c)=0 inconclusive; L(x) = f(a) + f'(a)(x - a); f(x) ~ L(x); dy = f'(x) dx; Δy ~ dy; f(x+Δx) ~ f(x) + dy; Error = Δy - dy; Relative error = (Δy-dy)/Δy; Percentage error = |(Δy-dy)/Δy| x 100; Optimisation steps: define variables, objective, constraint, critical points, verify with 2nd derivative, interpret

**Formulas / laws:** (x-h)^2 + (y-k)^2 = r^2; x = r cos θ, y = r sin θ; x^2 + y^2 + 2gx + 2fy + c = 0: centre (-g,-f), r = sqrt(g^2+f^2-c); Diameter endpoints: (x-x1)(x-x2) + (y-y1)(y-y2) = 0; Tangent to x^2+y^2=r^2 at (x1,y1): xx1 + yy1 = r^2; general: xx1 + yy1 + g(x+x1) + f(y+y1) + c = 0; Position: x1^2+y1^2+2gx1+2fy1+c >, =, < 0 (outside, on, inside); y = mx + c tangent to x^2+y^2=a^2 iff c^2 = a^2(1+m^2); point of contact (∓am/sqrt(1+m^2), ±a/sqrt(1+m^2)); Tangent length = sqrt(x1^2+y1^2+2gx1+2fy1+c); chord length = 2 sqrt(a^2 - d^2); Parabola y^2 = 4ax: focus (a,0), directrix x=-a, latus rectum 4a, parametric (at^2, 2at); other forms y^2=-4ax, x^2=±4ay; Tangency to parabola: c = a/m; tangent at (x1,y1): yy1 = 2a(x+x1); normal: y - y1 = -(y1/2a)(x - x1); Ellipse x^2/a^2 + y^2/b^2 = 1 (a>b): c^2 = a^2 - b^2, e = c/a < 1, directrices x = ±a/e, latus rectum 2b^2/a, parametric (a cos θ, b sin θ); Ellipse tangency: c^2 = a^2 m^2 + b^2; tangent xx1/a^2 + yy1/b^2 = 1; normal a^2x/x1 - b^2y/y1 = a^2 - b^2; Hyperbola x^2/a^2 - y^2/b^2 = 1: c^2 = a^2 + b^2, e = c/a > 1, asymptotes y = ±(b/a)x, latus rectum 2b^2/a, parametric (a sec θ, b tan θ); Hyperbola tangency: c^2 = a^2 m^2 - b^2; tangent xx1/a^2 - yy1/b^2 = 1; normal a^2x/x1 + b^2y/y1 = a^2 + b^2; Translated forms: (x-h)^2/a^2 ± (y-k)^2/b^2 = 1 etc.; Satellite orbit: e = (A - P)/(A + P)

**Formulas / laws:** LRT: f continuous on [a,b], f(a)f(b) < 0 => root in (a,b); Tolerance for n decimals: |f(c)| < 10^-n / 2 (approx. 0.005 for 2 d.p.); Bisection: c = (a+b)/2; Regula-Falsi: c = [a f(b) - b f(a)] / [f(b) - f(a)]; Newton–Raphson: x_(n+1) = x_n - f(x_n)/f'(x_n); Trapezium rule: ∫_a^b f(x)dx ~ (h/2)[f(x0) + 2(f(x1)+...+f(x_(n-1))) + f(xn)], h = (b-a)/n

**Formulas / laws:** Accuracy = (TP + TN) / (TP + TN + FP + FN); Precision = TP / (TP + FP); Recall = TP / (TP + FN); F1 = 2 x (Precision x Recall) / (Precision + Recall); Worked: TP=40, TN=50, FP=10, FN=5 -> Precision 0.8, Recall 0.8889, F1 0.8421; Typical split 80% train / 20% test; sklearn: train_test_split(X, y, random_state=42); LinearRegression().fit(); predict(); mean_squared_error(); matplotlib scatter/plot; Output: MSE 0.1484; Exam Score = 0.6552 x Hours Studied + 0.1466

**Formulas / laws:** t = (x_bar - mu0) / (s / sqrt(n)); example (80-75)/(10/sqrt25) = 2.5; chi^2 = sum (Oi - Ei)^2 / Ei; die rolled 60 times -> chi^2 = 3.4; F = s1^2 / s2^2; example 16/9 = 1.78; s = sqrt( sum (xi - x_bar)^2 / (n-1) ); df = n - 1; Numerical: marks 72,75,78,74,77,80,76,79,81,78 vs mu=70: mean 77, s~2.79, t~7.93, df=9, p~0.00002 < 0.05 -> reject H0; Decision: if p-value < alpha reject H0; else fail to reject; h_theta(x) = y (hypothesis function, PDF 92)
